B. {-\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}, 1}

\]

\]

\boxed{C}

Prompt
请解答以下数学题。逐步推理，将最终答案放在 \boxed{} 中。

6. 已知函数f(x)=\frac{x+2}{e^x+a}的最大值为1，则a = （ ）
A. \frac{1}{2}
B. 1
C. \frac{3}{2}
D. 2

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
由选项可知 \(a>0\)，所以分母 \(e^x+a>0\)。

若 \(f(x)=\dfrac{x+2}{e^x+a}\) 的最大值为 \(1\)，则对任意 \(x\) 都有

\[
\frac{x+2}{e^x+a}\le 1.
\]

因为 \(e^x+a>0\)，可化为

\[
x+2\le e^x+a,
\]

即

\[
a\ge x+2-e^x.
\]

令

\[
g(x)=x+2-e^x,
\]

则

\[
g'(x)=1-e^x.
\]

当 \(x<0\) 时 \(g'(x)>0\)，当 \(x>0\) 时 \(g'(x)<0\)，所以 \(g(x)\) 在 \(x=0\) 处取得最大值：

\[
g(0)=0+2-1=1.
\]

因此必须有

\[
a\ge 1.
\]

另一方面，因为最大值是 \(1\)，所以存在某个 \(x_0\) 使得

\[
f(x_0)=1.
\]

即

\[
\frac{x_0+2}{e^{x_0}+a}=1,
\]

得到

\[
a=x_0+2-e^{x_0}=g(x_0)\le 1.
\]

所以

\[
a=1.
\]

验证：当 \(a=1\) 时，

\[
f(x)=\frac{x+2}{e^x+1},
\]

且

\[
f(0)=1,
\]

同时由上面推导可知 \(f(x)\le 1\)，故最大值确为 \(1\)。

因此答案为

\[
\boxed{B}
\]
Reasoning
We need answer Chinese math multiple choice. Need solve. Function f(x)=(x+2)/(e^x+a), maximum is 1, find a among options. Need be careful domain? e^x+a denominator likely positive? a must ensure function defined for all real? If a>0 then denominator positive. If a=0, x=-ln? no e^x never zero so denominator e^x >0; actually a can be 0 okay. If a<0 denominator zero at e^x=-a -> x=ln(-a), undefined; maybe domain restricted? Usually function defined R implies a>=0? But maximum 1 maybe positive a.

We need find a such that max_{x in R} (x+2)/(e^x+a)=1. Options positive.

Set maximum equal 1. Since as x -> -inf, x+2 -> -inf, denominator -> a (if a>0), so f -> -inf? If a>0, yes f -> -inf. If a=0, denominator ->0+ and numerator -inf -> -inf (more). x->inf numerator~x, denominator~e^x, f->0+. So if a>0, f goes -inf at -inf, 0 at inf, has max positive maybe.

Derivative: f'(x) = [(1)(e^x+a) - (x+2)e^x]/(e^x+a)^2 = [e^x + a - (x+2)e^x]/... = [a - (x+1)e^x]/... . Critical when a = (x+1)e^x. For a>0, solutions? g(x)=(x+1)e^x. g min at x=-2: g(-2)=(-1)e^{-2}=-1/e^2; g(-1)=0; as x->-inf, g->0? x+1 negative, e^x ->0, product ->0-; as x->inf ->inf. So for 0<a? equation g(x)=a has unique x > -1? Since g increasing on [-1,inf). For a>0, unique critical x> -1. Since f' sign: denominator positive. f' sign = a - g(x). For x less than critical? g(x)<a? Need g tends 0- at -inf then up to 0 at -1, to inf. For a>0, g(x)<a for x < x_c, >a for x>x_c. Thus f increasing up to x_c, decreasing after; max at x_c.

At critical, a=(x+1)e^x. f(x_c) = (x+2)/(e^x + (x+1)e^x) = (x+2)/(e^x(1+x+1)) = (x+2)/(e^x(x+2)) = 1/e^x, provided x+2 not 0. If x=-2? Then a negative? g(-2)=-1/e^2. For a>0 x_c > -1, not -2. So max = e^{-x_c}. Set max=1 => e^{-x_c}=1 => x_c=0. Then a=(0+1)e^0=1. So answer B. Need check maybe a=?? If x_c=0, f(0)=2/(1+a), but critical condition a=1 => 1. Good.

Need perhaps consider if a not positive? The options all positive. We can present concise. But need ensure maximum is 1 not just local. For a=1, f(x)=(x+2)/(e^x+1). Derivative [1-(x+1)e^x]/...; max at x=0; value=1. Need prove global? Since derivative sign. Also f can approach 0 from below? Actually f at -inf -inf if a>0; at +inf 0+. It has local max 1. Since derivative sign + then -, global max 1. OK.

Could also solve by inequality: require f(x) ≤ 1 for all x and equality for some x. For a>0: (x+2)/(e^x+a) ≤1. If e^x+a>0. Equivalent x+2 ≤ e^x+a => x+2 - e^x ≤ a. Need minimal a such that sup_{x} (x+2-e^x) ≤ a? But maximum =1 imposes something. Let's check: f≤1 iff x+2 ≤ e^x+a (assuming positive denom) -> a ≥ x+2-e^x for all x. The maximum of h=x+2-e^x is? h'=1-e^x => max at x=0 value 1. Thus a ≥ 1. If maximum of f is exactly 1, also need existence f=1? If a>1, f<1 for all? Let's see h≤a, strict unless x=0 and a=1. If a>1 no equality; max <1 maybe. If a<1 h(0)=1>a so f(0)>1? If a positive. This simpler! Need be careful sign: If denominator positive. Options positive. If a>0, condition max=1 equivalent a=1. Prompt
请解答以下数学题。逐步推理，将最终答案放在 \boxed{} 中。

7. 一百零八塔位于宁夏回族自治区青铜峡市，以其独特的建筑格局和深厚的历史文化闻名遐迩。该塔群共有108座塔，依山势自上而下排成12行，将第i行中塔的座数记为a_i(i=1,2,...,12)，其中a_1=1, a_2=a_3=3, a_4=a_5=5，且a_6, a_7, ..., a_{12}是一个首项为7，公差为2的等差数列。将a_1, a_2, ..., a_{12}分为6组，每组2个数，使得每组的2个数之和可构成一个项数为6且公差为d(d>0)的等差数列，则d = （ ）
A. 2
B. 4
C. 6
D. 8

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
由题意可得

\[
(a_1,a_2,\dots,a_{12})=(1,3,3,5,5,7,9,11,13,15,17,19).
\]

这些数都是奇数，因此任意两个数配对，其和必为偶数。

设分成的 6 组的两数之和从小到大构成等差数列

\[
x,\ x+d,\ x+2d,\ x+3d,\ x+4d,\ x+5d.
\]

因为这 12 个数之和为 108，所以 6 个和的总和也为 108：

\[
x+(x+d)+(x+2d)+(x+3d)+(x+4d)+(x+5d)=108,
\]

即

\[
6x+15d=108.
\]

两边除以 3，得

\[
2x+5d=36.
\]

逐项检验选项：

- 若 \(d=2\)，则 \(2x+10=36\)，得 \(x=13\)，为奇数，但每对之和应为偶数，矛盾。
- 若 \(d=6\)，则 \(2x+30=36\)，得 \(x=3\)，但最小可能配对和为 \(1+3=4\)，矛盾。
- 若 \(d=8\)，则 \(2x+40=36\)，得 \(x=-2\)，不可能。
- 若 \(d=4\)，则 \(2x+20=36\)，得 \(x=8\)，可能。

验证 \(d=4\) 时确实可以配对：

\[
\begin{aligned}
1+7&=8,\\
9+3&=12,\\
13+3&=16,\\
15+5&=20,\\
19+5&=24,\\
17+11&=28.
\end{aligned}
\]

所得六个和为

\[
8,12,16,20,24,28,
\]

公差为 \(4\)。

因此

\[
d=4.
\]

\[
\boxed{B}
\]

Prompt
请解答以下数学题。逐步推理，将最终答案放在 \boxed{} 中。

8. 设U={(x_1, x_2, x_3)|x_i \in \{-2,-1,1,2\}, i=1,2,3}\}为空间中64个点构成的集合，点P(1,1,1)，记样本空间\Omega = C_U^P，从\Omega中随机取一个点，定义随机变量X如下：对\Omega中的每个点A(x_1, x_2, x_3)，令X(A) = x_1 + x_2 + x_3，则X的数学期望值为（ ）
A. -\frac{1}{21}
B. -\frac{1}{63}
C. 0
D. \frac{1}{7}

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
按题中记号，\(C_U^P\) 表示从集合 \(U\) 中除去点 \(P(1,1,1)\) 后得到的集合，即

\[
\Omega=U\setminus\{P\}.
\]

因为 \(U\) 中共有 \(4^3=64\) 个点，所以

\[
|\Omega|=64-1=63.
\]

先计算在全部 \(64\) 个点上，\(X=x_1+x_2+x_3\) 的总和。

对任意一个坐标，例如 \(x_1\)，其取值集合为 \(\{-2,-1,1,2\}\)，且

\[
-2+(-1)+1+2=0.
\]

对于固定的 \(x_1\)，其余两个坐标各有 \(4\) 种取法，因此

\[
\sum_{(x_1,x_2,x_3)\in U}x_1
=16\bigl((-2)+(-1)+1+2\bigr)=0.
\]

同理，

\[
\sum_{U}x_2=0,\qquad \sum_{U}x_3=0.
\]

所以在整个 \(U\) 上，

\[
\sum_{A\in U}X(A)=0.
\]

而点 \(P(1,1,1)\) 在 \(U\) 中，且

\[
X(P)=1+1+1=3.
\]

因此从 \(U\) 中去掉 \(P\) 后，剩下 \(63\) 个点的 \(X\) 值总和为

\[
0-3=-3.
\]

随机从 \(\Omega\) 中取一个点，故数学期望为

\[
E(X)=\frac{-3}{63}=-\frac{1}{21}.
\]

对应选项 A。

\[
\boxed{A}
\]

9. 设z = 3 + 2i，则（ ）
A. \bar{z} = 3 - 2i
B. |z| = 5
C. z^2 = 5 + 12i
D. \frac{z+3}{z-i} \in \mathbf{R}

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
逐項检验：

- \(z=3+2i\)

**A.**  
\[
\overline{z}=3-2i
\]
正确。

**B.**  
\[
|z|=\sqrt{3^2+2^2}=\sqrt{13}\neq 5
\]
错误。

**C.**  
\[
z^2=(3+2i)^2=9+12i+4i^2=9+12i-4=5+12i
\]
正确。

**D.**  
\[
\frac{z+3}{z-i}=\frac{6+2i}{2+i}
\]
有理化：
\[
\frac{6+2i}{2+i}\cdot\frac{2-i}{2-i}
=\frac{(6+2i)(2-i)}{5}
=\frac{14-2i}{5}
\]
虚部不为 \(0\)，所以不是实数，错误。

因此正确选项为 A 和 C。

\[
\boxed{AC}
\]

gaokao_000_009 ✓ BC BC Y Y 21489 47.2 455.7 http://192.168.0.41:9292
Prompt
请解答以下数学题。逐步推理，将最终答案放在 \boxed{} 中。

10. 在空间中，A、B为两个定点，动点C到直线AB的距离为2，动点D到直线AB的距离为1。若二面角C - AB - D为60°，则（ ）
A. \angle CAD \ge 60^\circ
B. CD \ge \sqrt{3}
C. 当AB \perp CD时，CD \perp平面ABD
D. 当AB \perp平面ACD时，AC \perp AD

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
设直线 \(AB\) 的方向单位向量为 \(\mathbf e\)，取 \(A\) 为原点。  
由于 \(C\) 到直线 \(AB\) 的距离为 \(2\)，\(D\) 到直线 \(AB\) 的距离为 \(1\)，且二面角 \(C-AB-D\) 的平面角为 \(60^\circ\)，可设

\[
\overrightarrow{AC}=x\mathbf e+2\mathbf u,\qquad 
\overrightarrow{AD}=y\mathbf e+\mathbf v,
\]

其中 \(\mathbf u,\mathbf v\perp \mathbf e\)，\(|\mathbf u|=|\mathbf v|=1\)，且

\[
\mathbf u\cdot \mathbf v=\cos 60^\circ=\frac12.
\]

---

### 判断 B

\[
\overrightarrow{CD}=\overrightarrow{AD}-\overrightarrow{AC}
=(y-x)\mathbf e+\mathbf v-2\mathbf u.
\]

因为 \(\mathbf e\perp \mathbf u,\mathbf v\)，所以

\[
\begin{aligned}
CD^2
&=(y-x)^2+|\mathbf v-2\mathbf u|^2\\
&=(y-x)^2+1+4-4(\mathbf u\cdot \mathbf v)\\
&=(y-x)^2+5-4\cdot\frac12\\
&=(y-x)^2+3\ge 3.
\end{aligned}
\]

因此

\[
CD\ge \sqrt3,
\]

故 B 正确。

---

### 判断 A

取 \(x=y=1\)，则

\[
\overrightarrow{AC}=\mathbf e+2\mathbf u,\qquad
\overrightarrow{AD}=\mathbf e+\mathbf v.
\]

此时

\[
\cos\angle CAD
=\frac{\overrightarrow{AC}\cdot\overrightarrow{AD}}
{|\overrightarrow{AC}|\,|\overrightarrow{AD}|}
=\frac{1+2(\mathbf u\cdot\mathbf v)}
{\sqrt5\sqrt2}
=\frac{2}{\sqrt{10}}>\frac12.
\]

所以

\[
\angle CAD<60^\circ,
\]

A 不一定成立，故 A 错误。

---

### 判断 C

若 \(AB\perp CD\)，则 \(\overrightarrow{CD}\perp \mathbf e\)，即

\[
(y-x)\mathbf e\cdot \mathbf e=y-x=0,
\]

所以 \(x=y\)。于是

\[
\overrightarrow{CD}=\mathbf v-2\mathbf u.
\]

又

\[
\overrightarrow{AD}=x\mathbf e+\mathbf v,
\]

因此

\[
\overrightarrow{CD}\cdot\overrightarrow{AD}
=(\mathbf v-2\mathbf u)\cdot \mathbf v
=1-2(\mathbf u\cdot\mathbf v)
=1-1=0.
\]

所以 \(CD\perp AD\)。又已知 \(CD\perp AB\)。  
在平面 \(ABD\) 内，过 \(D\) 作 \(AB\) 的平行线，则 \(CD\) 同时垂直于该线与 \(AD\)，二者交于 \(D\)，故

\[
CD\perp \text{平面 }ABD.
\]

因此 C 正确。

---

### 判断 D

若 \(AB\perp\) 平面 \(ACD\)，则 \(C,D\) 都在过 \(A\) 且垂直于 \(AB\) 的平面内，因此 \(x=y=0\)。此时

\[
\overrightarrow{AC}=2\mathbf u,\qquad
\overrightarrow{AD}=\mathbf v.
\]

于是

\[
\cos\angle CAD
=\frac{2\mathbf u\cdot\mathbf v}{2\cdot1}
=\mathbf u\cdot\mathbf v
=\frac12,
\]

所以

\[
\angle CAD=60^\circ\neq 90^\circ.
\]

故 D 错误。

综上，正确选项为

\[
\boxed{BC}
\]
Reasoning

A. angle CAD >= 60°
B. CD >= sqrt(3)
C. When AB ⟂ CD, CD ⟂ plane ABD
D. When AB ⟂ plane ACD, AC ⟂ AD

Dihedral angle between half-planes containing AB and C, and AB and D is 60°. This means angle between projections of C and D onto plane perpendicular to AB? Let's formulate.

Let's think about if option C conclusion "CD ⟂ 平面ABD" maybe requires CD intersect plane ABD? A line perpendicular to a plane usually must intersect it. Does CD intersect plane ABD? If CD is perpendicular to two lines in plane, but if it does not intersect plane, can we say perpendicular to plane? In Euclidean geometry, a line perpendicular to a plane is defined as line intersecting the plane and perpendicular to every line in the plane through the intersection point. If a line is parallel to a normal vector but not intersecting, it's often called perpendicular to the plane if it intersects? In Chinese geometry, "直线垂直于平面" requires line intersects plane? Usually: if a line is perpendicular to two intersecting lines in a plane, then it is perpendicular to the plane. This theorem assumes the line intersects the plane? Let's recall: 判定定理：如果一条直线与平面内的两条相交直线都垂直，则该直线与这个平面垂直. It might implicitly require the line and the two lines intersect? Actually "一条直线与一个平面内的两条相交直线都垂直" means the line intersects those two lines? In space, two lines can be skew, "垂直" for skew lines often means direction perpendicular? In high school, "异面直线垂直" can be defined by angle 90, not necessarily intersecting. The theorem often: If a line is perpendicular to two intersecting lines in a plane, then the line is perpendicular to the plane. It does not explicitly require the line intersects the plane? But if it is perpendicular to two intersecting lines in plane (as lines, maybe skew), then its direction is normal to plane. It may be parallel to a normal line; to be "perpendicular to plane" maybe it should be intersect? Let's check: In Chinese textbooks, 直线与平面垂直: 如果直线l与平面α内的任意一条直线都垂直，则称l与α垂直. This does not require intersection? Actually if l is parallel to a normal but not in plane, is it perpendicular to every line in plane? For skew lines, angle defined by translating, so yes. But some definitions require l intersects α? Let's see: "如果一条直线和一个平面内的任意一条直线都垂直，则这条直线和这个平面互相垂直" The line may not intersect? In Euclidean geometry, a line perpendicular to a plane must intersect it; but the condition with every line in plane would force intersection? Example: line l parallel to normal not intersecting plane: it is perpendicular to every line in plane? They might not consider because no intersection? But if l is skew to m,n, can we say l⊥m? In space, two lines are perpendicular if the angle between them is 90°, including skew? Yes, "异面直线垂直" is a thing. So theorem can apply.

In our case, CD and AB are skew (unless CD passes E). Are they considered perpendicular? The condition says AB⊥CD, so yes. To prove plane perpendicular, we can use line through D parallel to AB, which intersects CD at D. Since CD⊥AB, it is also perpendicular to any line parallel to AB. Let l be through D parallel to AB. l lies in plane ABD? Plane ABD contains AB and D; the line through D parallel to AB is in the plane (because a plane containing a line contains all parallel lines through a point in the plane? Yes, if D is in plane and AB direction lies in plane, the parallel through D is in plane). l intersects AD at D. CD⊥l and CD⊥AD. Therefore by theorem (with intersections at D) CD⊥planeABD. This is bulletproof. Let's include maybe in vector proof a note.

Let's verify CD⊥AD in C when x=y but using CD vector from C to D = v-2u. If x not 0, AD=x e+v. Dot zero. Geometric: CD⊥DE? We need AD not DE. Does CD⊥AD always? We proved. Geometric: In plane perpendicular to AB at E, CD⊥DE. But AD has component AE along AB plus DE. Since CD⊥AB, CD·AE=0, and CD⊥DE, so CD⊥AD. Good.

Now, consider if x=y but D's foot E not between A and B? Doesn't matter. Plane ABD includes line through D parallel AB. Good.

Now D: If AB⊥plane ACD, then plane ACD is perpendicular to AB at A. Does that imply C and D's feet are A? Yes. Then angle between AC and AD is the plane angle of the dihedral? Let's verify: Dihedral C-AB-D: In cross-section plane perpendicular to AB at A (which is plane ACD), the faces intersect in rays AC and AD. The angle between those rays is 60. Thus AC and AD angle 60. So D false. Good.

\vec{CD}=v-2u,
\vec{AD}=x e+v,
\vec{CD}·\vec{AD}=1-2(u·v)=0,
所以CD⊥AD。又CD⊥AB；在平面ABD内过D作AB的平行线，CD垂直于该线和AD（两线相交于D），故CD⊥平面ABD。C正确。

(D) 若AB⊥平面ACD，则C,D都在过A且垂直AB的平面内，即x=y=0。此时
cos∠CAD = \frac{\vec{AC}·\vec{AD}}{|AC||AD|}=u·v=1/2,
所以∠CAD=60°，不是90°。D错。

Prompt
请解答以下数学题。逐步推理，将最终答案放在 \boxed{} 中。

11. 已知圆C_1:(x+1)^2+y^2=1，圆C_2:(x-1)^2+y^2=1，圆C_3:x^2+(y-\sqrt{3})^2=1，直线l:y=kx+b与C_1, C_2, C_3均有两个交点。记l被C_1, C_2, C_3截得的弦长分别为s_1、s_2、s_3，则（ ）
A. k可以取任意实数
B. 满足s_1=s_2=s_3的直线l共有3条
C. 满足s_1+s_2+s_3=3的直线l多于3条
D. 当b=0时，s_1+s_2+s_3的最大值为\frac{2\sqrt{21}}{3}

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
设  
\[
q=\sqrt{1+k^2}.
\]
三个圆心分别为  
\[
O_1(-1,0),\quad O_2(1,0),\quad O_3(0,\sqrt3).
\]
直线 \(l:y=kx+b\) 可写为 \(kx-y+b=0\)，所以三圆心到直线的距离为  
\[
d_1=\frac{|b-k|}{q},\qquad d_2=\frac{|b+k|}{q},\qquad d_3=\frac{|b-\sqrt3|}{q}.
\]
因为三个圆半径都是 \(1\)，且直线与每个圆均有两个交点，故 \(d_i<1\)，弦长为  
\[
s_i=2\sqrt{1-d_i^2}\qquad (i=1,2,3).
\]

---

### A 选项

取 \(k=\frac1{\sqrt3}\)，则 \(q=\frac2{\sqrt3}\)。  
要有两个交点，需要

\[
|b-\tfrac1{\sqrt3}|<\tfrac2{\sqrt3},
\]
\[
|b+\tfrac1{\sqrt3}|<\tfrac2{\sqrt3},
\]
\[
|b-\sqrt3|<\tfrac2{\sqrt3}.
\]

第一个条件给出  
\[
b\in\left(-\frac1{\sqrt3},\sqrt3\right),
\]
第二个条件给出  
\[
b\in\left(-\sqrt3,\frac1{\sqrt3}\right),
\]
所以前两个条件合起来只有  
\[
b\in\left(-\frac1{\sqrt3},\frac1{\sqrt3}\right).
\]

第三个条件给出  
\[
b\in\left(\frac1{\sqrt3},\frac5{\sqrt3}\right).
\]

两者没有公共部分（端点 \(b=\frac1{\sqrt3}\) 时与 \(C_2,C_3\) 相切，不满足“两个交点”）。因此并非任意实数 \(k\) 都可以，A 错。

---

### B 选项

半径相同，弦长相等当且仅当圆心到直线的距离相等，即  
\[
d_1=d_2=d_3.
\]

三个圆心构成边长为 \(2\) 的等边三角形。若一条直线到三个顶点的距离相等，则这条直线必平行于三角形的一条边，并且位于该边与对顶点连线的中点处。等边三角形共有三条边，因此这样的直线共有 \(3\) 条。

具体为三条中位线：

\[
y=\frac{\sqrt3}{2},\qquad y=\sqrt3\,x,\qquad y=-\sqrt3\,x.
\]

它们到三个圆心的距离都是 \(\frac{\sqrt3}{2}<1\)，确实与每个圆有两个交点。故 B 对。

---

### C 选项

由 B 可知，上述 \(3\) 条直线满足
\[
s_1=s_2=s_3=2\sqrt{1-\frac34}=1,
\]
所以
\[
s_1+s_2+s_3=3.
\]

再找一条不同的直线。考虑水平线 \(y=t\)，即 \(k=0\)。  
要同时与三个圆相交，需
\[
\sqrt3-1<t<1.
\]
此时
\[
s_1=s_2=2\sqrt{1-t^2},\qquad s_3=2\sqrt{1-(t-\sqrt3)^2}.
\]
令
\[
F(t)=s_1+s_2+s_3
=4\sqrt{1-t^2}+2\sqrt{1-(t-\sqrt3)^2}.
\]

取端点附近的值：
\[
F(\sqrt3-1)=4\sqrt{2\sqrt3-3}\approx 2.724<3.
\]
取 \(t=\frac45\)：
\[
F\left(\frac45\right)
=\frac{12}{5}+\frac25\sqrt{40\sqrt3-66}
\approx 3.124>3.
\]

由连续性，存在 \(t_0\in(\sqrt3-1,\frac45)\)，使得
\[
F(t_0)=3.
\]
这条水平线 \(y=t_0\) 与 B 中的三条直线都不同，因此满足 \(s_1+s_2+s_3=3\) 的直线多于 \(3\) 条。故 C 对。

---

### D 选项

当 \(b=0\) 时，直线为 \(y=kx\)。设
\[
q=\sqrt{1+k^2}.
\]
此时
\[
d_1=d_2=\frac{|k|}{q},\qquad d_3=\frac{\sqrt3}{q}.
\]
要与三圆均有两个交点，需
\[
\frac{\sqrt3}{q}<1\quad\Longrightarrow\quad q>\sqrt3.
\]

弦长和为
\[
S=s_1+s_2+s_3
=\frac4q+2\sqrt{1-\frac3{q^2}}.
\]

令
\[
x=\frac1q\in\left(0,\frac1{\sqrt3}\right),
\]
则
\[
S=4x+2\sqrt{1-3x^2}.
\]

设
\[
u=\sqrt3\,x,\qquad v=\sqrt{1-3x^2},
\]
则
\[
u^2+v^2=1,
\]
且
\[
S=\frac4{\sqrt3}u+2v.
\]
由柯西不等式，
\[
S\le \sqrt{\left(\frac4{\sqrt3}\right)^2+2^2}\sqrt{u^2+v^2}
=\sqrt{\frac{16}{3}+4}
=\sqrt{\frac{28}{3}}
=\frac{2\sqrt{21}}{3}.
\]

等号可取到：当
\[
\frac{u}{v}=\frac{2}{\sqrt3},\qquad u^2+v^2=1
\]
时，得
\[
u=\frac2{\sqrt7},\quad v=\frac{\sqrt3}{\sqrt7},
\]
从而
\[
x=\frac{u}{\sqrt3}=\frac2{\sqrt{21}},
\]
即
\[
q=\frac{\sqrt{21}}2,\qquad k^2=q^2-1=\frac{21}{4}-1=\frac{17}{4},
\]
确实在允许范围内。

所以 \(b=0\) 时最大值确为
\[
\frac{2\sqrt{21}}{3}.
\]
故 D 对。

---

综上，正确选项为

\[
\boxed{BCD}
\]

12. 双曲线5x^2 - 6y^2 = 1的离心率为__________.

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
将双曲线方程化为标准形式：

\[
5x^2-6y^2=1
\]

两边除以 1：

\[
\frac{x^2}{\frac15}-\frac{y^2}{\frac16}=1
\]

所以

\[
a^2=\frac15,\qquad b^2=\frac16
\]

双曲线中

\[
c^2=a^2+b^2
\]

因此

\[
c^2=\frac15+\frac16=\frac{11}{30}
\]

离心率

\[
e=\frac ca=\sqrt{\frac{c^2}{a^2}}
=\sqrt{\frac{\frac{11}{30}}{\frac15}}
=\sqrt{\frac{11}{30}\cdot 5}
=\sqrt{\frac{11}{6}}
\]

所以答案为

\[
\boxed{\sqrt{\frac{11}{6}}}
\]
Reasoning
We need answer math problem in Chinese. Need solve: hyperbola 5x^2 - 6y^2 = 1 eccentricity. Need put final in boxed. Let's analyze carefully.

Equation 5x^2 - 6y^2 = 1. Standard form x^2/(1/5) - y^2/(1/6) = 1. For hyperbola x^2/a^2 - y^2/b^2 = 1, a^2=1/5, b^2=1/6. c^2=a^2+b^2=1/5+1/6=11/30. Eccentricity e=c/a = sqrt(c^2/a^2)= sqrt((11/30)/(1/5)) = sqrt(11/30 *5)=sqrt(55/30)=sqrt(11/6). Or e = sqrt(1+b^2/a^2) = sqrt(1+ (1/6)/(1/5))=sqrt(1+5/6)=sqrt(11/6). Is that answer? Could rationalize sqrt(66)/6? sqrt(11/6)=sqrt66/6? sqrt(11/6)=sqrt(66)/6 yes because multiply numerator denominator by 6: sqrt(66)/6. Usually answer sqrt(11/6). Need final boxed.

Need ensure no mistake: 5x^2 - 6y^2 = 1 -> x^2/(1/5) - y^2/(1/6) =1. Yes transverse axis x. Eccentricity >1: sqrt(11/6)=sqrt 1.833=1.354. Good.

Need provide step-by-step reasoning. Final answer in \boxed{}. Chinese.
Grader reason
The computed eccentricity sqrt(11/6) is equivalent to the expected sqrt(66)/6, and the final answer is correctly placed in \boxed{}.

gaokao_000_012 ✓ \frac{3\pi}{2}, 1 \theta=\frac{3\pi}{2},\quad f\left(\frac{2\pi}{3}\right)=1 Y Y 8773 46.8 187.6 http://192.168.0.41:9292
Prompt
请解答以下数学题。逐步推理，将最终答案放在 \boxed{} 中。

13. 已知f(x)=2sin(ax+\theta) (a \in Z, 0 \le \theta < 2\pi)是偶函数，f(x)在区间(0, \frac{\pi}{2})单调递增。则\theta =__________，f(\frac{2\pi}{3}) =__________.

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
解：

因为 \(f(x)=2\sin(ax+\theta)\) 是偶函数，所以对任意 \(x\)，有

\[
2\sin(ax+\theta)=2\sin(-ax+\theta).
\]

即

\[
\sin(\theta+ax)-\sin(\theta-ax)=2\cos\theta\sin(ax)=0.
\]

若 \(a=0\)，则 \(f(x)\) 为常数，不是严格单调递增，因此 \(a\neq 0\)。于是必须

\[
\cos\theta=0,
\]

所以

\[
\theta=\frac{\pi}{2}\quad \text{或}\quad \theta=\frac{3\pi}{2}.
\]

---

### 1. 排除 \(\theta=\frac{\pi}{2}\)

若 \(\theta=\frac{\pi}{2}\)，则

\[
f(x)=2\sin\left(ax+\frac{\pi}{2}\right)=2\cos(ax).
\]

令 \(n=|a|\)，则 \(f(x)=2\cos(nx)\)。在 \(x>0\) 的足够小区间内，\(\cos(nx)\) 随 \(x\) 增大而减小，所以 \(f(x)\) 在该区间内单调递减，与题设矛盾。

因此

\[
\theta\neq \frac{\pi}{2}.
\]

---

### 2. 取 \(\theta=\frac{3\pi}{2}\)

此时

\[
f(x)=2\sin\left(ax+\frac{3\pi}{2}\right)=-2\cos(ax).
\]

因为 \(\cos\) 是偶函数，所以

\[
f(x)=-2\cos(|a|x).
\]

设 \(n=|a|\in \mathbb{N}^*\)，则

\[
f(x)=-2\cos(nx).
\]

要使 \(f(x)\) 在 \(\left(0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right)\) 上单调递增，需要 \(nx\) 在 \(\left(0,\frac{n\pi}{2}\right)\) 内不越过 \(\pi\)，即

\[
\frac{n\pi}{2}\le \pi,
\]

所以

\[
n\le 2.
\]

因此

\[
|a|=1 \quad \text{或} \quad |a|=2.
\]

---

于是

\[
f(x)=-2\cos(|a|x).
\]

计算

\[
f\left(\frac{2\pi}{3}\right)=-2\cos\left(|a|\cdot \frac{2\pi}{3}\right).
\]

若 \(|a|=1\)，则

\[
f\left(\frac{2\pi}{3}\right)=-2\cos\frac{2\pi}{3}=-2\left(-\frac12\right)=1.
\]

若 \(|a|=2\)，则

\[
f\left(\frac{2\pi}{3}\right)=-2\cos\frac{4\pi}{3}=-2\left(-\frac12\right)=1.
\]

所以最终答案为

\[
\boxed{\theta=\frac{3\pi}{2},\quad f\left(\frac{2\pi}{3}\right)=1}
\]
Reasoning
We need solve math problem. Need provide final answer boxed. Let's analyze carefully.

Prompt
请解答以下数学题。逐步推理，将最终答案放在 \boxed{} 中。

14. 设实数q满足：存在数列{a_n}，使得对于任意n \in N^*，均有a_1 + a_2 + \cdots + a_{3n} = n^2 + n，且{a_n}中有某连续9项a_k, a_{k+1}, \cdots, a_{k+8}是公比为q的等比数列。则q的最大值为__________.

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
解：

记
\[
B_n=a_{3n-2}+a_{3n-1}+a_{3n}.
\]
由题设
\[
a_1+a_2+\cdots+a_{3n}=n^2+n,
\]
得
\[
B_n=(n^2+n)-((n-1)^2+(n-1))=2n.
\]
也就是说，每连续 \(3\) 项分成一块，第 \(n\) 块的块和必须为 \(2n\)。

设连续 \(9\) 项为
\[
a_{k+j}=Aq^j\qquad(j=0,1,\dots,8).
\]
则任意连续 \(3\) 项之和为
\[
a_{k+j}+a_{k+j+1}+a_{k+j+2}
=Aq^j(1+q+q^2).
\]
由于
\[
1+q+q^2=\left(q+\frac12\right)^2+\frac34>0,
\]
若某两个完整块分别对应等比数列中相邻的两段 \(3\) 项，则这两块块和之比为
\[
q^3.
\]

现在按起点 \(k\) 的位置分三类讨论。

### 1. 起点为某块首项

若 \(k=3m-2\)，则这 \(9\) 项恰好是第 \(m,m+1,m+2\) 三整块。三块块和应分别为
\[
A(1+q+q^2),\quad Aq^3(1+q+q^2),\quad Aq^6(1+q+q^2),
\]
即成等比数列。但由块和条件，它们又应为
\[
2m,\quad 2m+2,\quad 2m+4.
\]
而
\[
(2m+2)^2\neq (2m)(2m+4)
\]
因为等价于 \((m+1)^2=m(m+2)\)，即 \(1=0\)，矛盾。因此这种起点不可能。

### 2. 起点为某块第 \(2\) 项或第 \(3\) 项

若 \(k=3m-1\) 或 \(k=3m\)，则这 \(9\) 项中恰好有第 \(m+1\) 块和第 \(m+2\) 块是完整块。记
\[
r=m+1,
\]
则 \(r\ge 2\)。

这两整块的块和分别是等比数列中相邻两个 \(3\) 项段之和，因此其比值为 \(q^3\)。另一方面，由块和条件，它们应为
\[
2r,\quad 2(r+1).
\]
所以
\[
q^3=\frac{2(r+1)}{2r}=\frac{r+1}{r}.
\]
因为 \(r\ge 2\)，
\[
q^3=\frac{r+1}{r}=1+\frac1r\le 1+\frac12=\frac32.
\]
又 \(q^3>0\)，故 \(q>0\)，于是
\[
q\le \sqrt[3]{\frac32}.
\]

### 3. 上界可达

取
\[
q_0=\sqrt[3]{\frac32}.
\]
令
\[
c=1+q_0+q_0^2,\qquad A=\frac{4}{q_0^2c}.
\]
定义
\[
a_{2+j}=Aq_0^j\qquad(j=0,1,\dots,8),
\]
即
\[
a_2,\dots,a_{10}
\]
是公比为 \(q_0\) 的等比数列。

再补充其余项：
\[
a_1=2-A(1+q_0),\qquad a_{11}=0,\qquad a_{12}=8-Aq_0^8.
\]
对 \(n\ge 5\)，取
\[
a_{3n-2}=2n,\qquad a_{3n-1}=0,\qquad a_{3n}=0.
\]

检查块和：
\[
a_1+a_2+a_3=2,
\]
\[
a_4+a_5+a_6=Aq_0^2(1+q_0+q_0^2)=4,
\]
\[
a_7+a_8+a_9=Aq_0^5(1+q_0+q_0^2)=4q_0^3=6,
\]
\[
a_{10}+a_{11}+a_{12}=Aq_0^8+0+(8-Aq_0^8)=8.
\]
且对 \(n\ge5\)，块和为 \(2n\)。因此所有块和都为 \(2n\)，从而
\[
a_1+\cdots+a_{3n}=\sum_{i=1}^n 2i=n^2+n.
\]
所以 \(q_0\) 确实满足条件。

故 \(q\) 的最大值为
\[
\boxed{\sqrt[3]{\frac32}}
\]
Reasoning
We need solve math problem. Need think fully. Let's restate.

Problem in Chinese:
14. Let real q satisfy: there exists a sequence {a_n}, such that for any n∈N*, a_1 + ... + a_{3n} = n^2 + n, and among {a_n} there are some consecutive 9 terms a_k, a_{k+1}, ..., a_{k+8} forming a geometric progression with common ratio q. Then the maximum value of q is ______.

We need find maximum real q for which there exists sequence a_n (real terms? likely real) satisfying partial sums over blocks of 3 length: S_{3n}=n^2+n for all positive integers n, and has 9 consecutive terms (somewhere) being geometric with ratio q. Need max q.

We need analyze constraints. Sequence arbitrary reals? Conditions only on sums of first 3n terms. This fixes sums of each block of three consecutive terms? Let's derive.
Let S_m = sum_{i=1}^m a_i. Given S_{3n}=n(n+1). For n-1: S_{3n-3}=(n-1)n. Thus block sum B_n = a_{3n-2}+a_{3n-1}+a_{3n}= n^2+n - (n-1)n = 2n. For n>=1, B_1=2, B_2=4, B_3=6, etc. So every block of three consecutive positions indexed by n has sum 2n. Within each block arbitrary reals summing to 2n.
Additionally, there exist k, 9 consecutive terms form GP: a_{k+j}=A q^j for j=0..8? If q maybe zero? Geometric ratio real. Need maximum q, so positive likely. Could q be negative? max maybe positive. Need constraints from block sums.

The 9 consecutive terms can start at arbitrary k, crossing block boundaries. Let block index positions: block n positions 3n-2,3n-1,3n. A run of 9 terms can start at offsets 1,2,0 mod 3 relative to blocks. We need find possible q values for which there exist some A, starting position k, and assignment to blocks satisfying block sums. Since only sums of 3 consecutive positions from original start, not sliding triple sums. Within those blocks, some positions are determined by GP, other positions arbitrary. We only need each involved block's total sum fixed 2m. Since arbitrary real, no restrictions except for blocks fully covered by 9 terms? If all 3 positions in a block are GP, then their sum must equal 2m. But there is also global sequence: arbitrary elsewhere. Take k=2? Start at second position of block 1 (n=1): k=2. Then 9 terms a_2...a_10 are GP with ratio q. Full blocks: block2 (positions 4,5,6) = terms a_4,a_5,a_6 correspond to b2,b3,b4? But we should verify q is maximum over real q. Need rigorous upper bound: Suppose such sequence exists. Let the 9 GP terms be a_{k+j}=A q^j (j=0,...,8). Since q real and A real. If A=0? Then all zero; but the run contains at least two full blocks of original 3-block partition; each full block sum would be 0, contradicting block sums 2m>0. So A≠0. Need show q>0? Since full block sums ratio positive, q^3 positive -> q>0. But for upper bound, q≤(3/2)^{1/3}. We can do:
The 9 consecutive positions must contain at least two complete blocks of the partition {3m-2,3m-1,3m}. Let the first of these complete blocks be block r (i.e., positions 3r-2,3r-1,3r) and the next be block r+1. (Need prove r≥2.) The terms in these two blocks are consecutive 3-term chunks of the GP, so their sums are A q^s(1+q+q^2) and A q^{s+3}(1+q+q^2) for some s∈{0,1,2}. Their ratio is q^3 (since 1+q+q^2>0 for real q and A q^s≠0). But the required sums are 2r and 2(r+1), ratio (r+1)/r. Therefore q^3=(r+1)/r. Also r≥2 because if r=1 then the GP run must start at position 1? Need a clear argument. Let's prove r≥2 for any length-9 interval with at least two full blocks not including three? Hmm if r=1, the first full block is block1. The 9-term interval contains block1 positions 1-3. Since start k≤1 (to include pos1) and k≥1, so k=1. Then it also contains positions 1-9, i.e. blocks 1,2,3 full. Then it would contain two more full blocks; the ratio of block2 and block3 sums would also have to equal q^3, leading contradiction? Actually for upper bound, if r=1, maybe not feasible at all, but to bound q we can still say if r=1 then also block3 full, giving q^3=(r+1)/r=2 and q^3=(r+2)/(r+1)=3/2, impossible. Thus no sequence with r=1. Alternatively simply because in any feasible sequence with at least two full blocks, the first full block cannot be block1 unless aligned causing three full blocks impossible; so r≥2. But for upper bound, we can handle r=1 by showing impossible or that q^3 would need be both 2 and 3/2. More elegantly: If the first full block is block1, since 9 terms include positions 1-3 and length 9, k=1, so the GP includes blocks 1,2,3. - Note for any i, x_i+x_{i+1}+x_{i+2}=A q^i(1+q+q^2). Since 1+q+q^2>0, any two consecutive such triples (i and i+3) have ratio q^3, provided nonzero (they are block sums, nonzero).
- Now analyze location of the 9 terms relative to blocks.
  * If they start at the first term of a block, they cover three whole blocks. The sums of these three blocks would have to be geometric with ratio q^3, but 2m,2m+2,2m+4 are not geometric (ratios differ), contradiction. Hence this cannot happen.
  * Therefore they start at second or third term of a block. Then they cover exactly two whole blocks, which are consecutive; let their indices be r and r+1, with r≥2 (because if starting in block 1 at second/third, r=2; starting later r larger). The sums of these two whole blocks are, in the GP, two consecutive triples, so their ratio is q^3. But the required sums are 2r and 2(r+1). Thus q^3=(r+1)/r.
- Since r≥2, q^3≤3/2. Also q^3>0, so q≤cube root.
- Construction for equality.
